- **mechanism**: Build an inspectable model of a real object from the evidence (spec sheets, teardowns, official photos, published test vectors), take it apart, and render a film with designed sound, an interactive viewer and an STL/GLB. Every number is tagged SPEC, MEASURED, DERIVED or ESTIMATED, and a spec whose numbers do not add up is not rendered. Use for exploded views, teardowns, comparisons and explainers of real products or machines (gear trains, key switches, cipher machines, phones, boards). - **mechanism-audit**: Audit the **mechanistic experiment rigor** for a specific claim. Catalogue currently has six slots A–F: A (steering coefficient sweep) is implemented; B–F are reserved for future checks (direction extraction quality, site/layer selection, n_effective sufficiency, probe-vs-causal disentanglement, intervention scope). Uses cross-model review (external LLM reviewer via llm-chat MCP). Complementary to `/experiment-audit` (which audits evaluation methodology, not mechanism tuning). - **mechanism-audit**: Audit a codebase for duplicated mechanism and for mechanism living in the wrong layer. Use when the user asks to find duplicated functionality, duplicate or parallel implementations, "two systems doing the same job", when planning to consolidate subsystems, or before implementing something that may already exist. Covers both helper-level duplication and the architectural case: two complex systems solving one problem, reached from different call sites, which no lexical duplicate detector can see. Produces a ranked, evidence-first report for downstream fixer agents. Strictly read-only. ([rigplane/rigplane-core](https://github.com/rigplane/rigplane-core/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/mechanism-audit) · ★30)
